Page No.# 266/267
O R D E R
01.11.2021
Heard Mr. A. R. Bhuyan, the learned counsel appearing for the petitioners in
WP(C)/3828/2018, Mr. S. K. Das, the leaned counsel for the petitioner in
WP(C)/5119/2019 and Mr. S. Islam, the learned counsel for the applicant in I.A.
(Civil)/1406/2021.
It is submitted by Mr. J. Handique, the learned Standing Counsel appearing
for the Revenue Department that in WP(C)/8394/2019, the Notification dated
10.04.2018 issued by the Government of Assam in the Revenue and Disaster
Management Department, canceling the earlier notification dated 06.01.2016
and amend the existing provision of Executive Instructions contained in Assam
Land Revenue Regulation, 1886 in regards to minimum qualification duties and
responsibilities and capacity-building of Gaonburas in Assam specified therein is
challenged. Accordingly, he requires three weeks time in order to file affidavit-in-opposition.
In view of the same, as the connected matters are also listed along with the
same, as such let all these matters be listed on 29.11.2021.
It is submitted by the learned counsel that various interim orders are
operating in various of the writ petitions. On the other hand, it is submitted by
Mr. Islam that a stay vacating petition is also pending in WP(C)/5119/2019
wherein it is submitted by Mr. Das that objection had already been filed.
In view of the submissions of the learned counsel, let these matters be
listed on 29.11.2021 on which date keeping in view the subsequent
developments as stated by Mr. Bhuyan, liberty is also granted to file additional

Page No.# 267/267
affidavit in WP(C)/3828/2018.
Interim order passed earlier in WP(C)/5119/2019 and WP(C)/6447/2019 is
extended till the next date.
 JUDGE
Comparing Assistant
